self = <app.strategy.engine.StrategyEngine object at 0x000002407F4EE4E0>
timeframe = 'M15'

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
    
        candles = series.candles
>       min_needed = self._config.swing_config.left_candles + self._config.swing_config.right_candles + 1
                     ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

timeframe = 'M15'

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return

    async def test_rejected_inputs_are_captured_structurally(self, monkeypatch):
        """A validator rejection is reported, not left only in a log line."""
        from app.strategy.engine import StrategyEngine
    
        original = StrategyEngine.evaluate_timeframe_once
        message = (
            "Fibonacci calculation rejected: Swing high (1.0) must be greater "
            "than swing low (2.0)"
        )
    
        async def patched(self, timeframe):  # noqa: ANN001
            await original(self, timeframe)
            state = self.get_timeframe_state(timeframe)
            if state is not None:
                state.last_error = message
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(StrategyEngine, "evaluate_timeframe_once", patched)

    def strategy_fingerprint(settings: Settings) -> str:
        """A stable fingerprint of the strategy + risk configuration in use.
    
        Two runs that share this fingerprint used exactly the same strategy and risk
        settings, so their reports are directly comparable. Timestamps and the
        candle data are deliberately excluded.
        """
        config = build_strategy_config(settings)
        policy = RiskPolicy.from_settings(settings)
        payload = repr(
            (
                settings.app_version,

self = <app.strategy.engine.StrategyEngine object at 0x000002407EF00AA0>
timeframe = 'M15'

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
    
        candles = series.candles

    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_start_equity_affects_risk_budget(self):
        """Lower start equity means smaller risk budgets."""

        engine_high = BacktestEngine(
            symbol="XAUUSD",
            timeframe="M15",
            candles=candles,

            min_candles=7,

timeframe = 'M15'

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return

    @pytest.mark.asyncio

    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_custom_profit_calculator(self):
        """Custom profit calculator should be used."""
        def custom_calc_profit(symbol, direction, volume, entry, exit_price):
            base = default_calc_profit(symbol, direction, volume, entry, exit_price)
            if base is None:
                return None
            return base * 2  # Double the profit for testing
    
        candles = cf.series_from_rows(simple_bullish_rows(20))
        start_utc = NOW
        end_utc = NOW + timedelta(minutes=1150)
    
        engine = BacktestEngine(
            symbol="XAUUSD",
            timeframe="M15",
            candles=candles,
            start_utc=start_utc,
            end_utc=end_utc,
            settings=make_settings(),
            calc_profit=custom_calc_profit,
            min_candles=7,
        )

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""

    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    
    async def test_snapshot_matches_a_truncated_history_oracle_at_every_index(
        self, make_settings
    ):

        settings, service = make_service(candles, make_settings)
        session = await create(service, candles)
        config = build_strategy_config(settings)
    
        # Nothing at all is visible before the first step.

timeframe = 'M15'

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
    
        candles = series.candles

    async def test_a_replay_failure_does_not_stop_live_evaluation(self, make_settings):
        settings = make_settings()
        candles = sc.impulse_candles()
        live_engine = StrategyEngine(
            sc.StubMonitor(candles), settings, build_strategy_config(settings)
        )
        await live_engine.evaluate_all_timeframes()
        before = live_fingerprint(live_engine)
    
        service = ReplayService(sc.StubMonitor(candles), settings)
        session = await service.create_session(
            timeframe="M15",
            start_utc=candles[0].open_time_utc,
            end_utc=sc.window_end(candles),
        )
    
        async def boom(timeframe):
            raise RuntimeError("injected replay failure")
    
        session.engine.evaluate_timeframe_once = boom  # type: ignore[method-assign]
        await service.step(session.session_id)
    
        assert session.status is ReplayStatus.FAILED
        assert "Strategy evaluation failed" in session.error
        assert service.snapshot(session)["results"]["completed"] is False
        # The live engine is unaffected and still evaluates normally.
        assert live_fingerprint(live_engine) == before
        await live_engine.evaluate_all_timeframes()

    async def test_concurrent_sessions_are_fully_isolated(self, make_settings):
        settings = make_settings()
        bullish = sc.impulse_candles()
        bearish = sc.impulse_invalidation_candles()

        session_a = await service.create_session(
            timeframe="M15",
            start_utc=bullish[0].open_time_utc,
            end_utc=sc.window_end(bullish),
        )
        session_b = await ReplayService(sc.StubMonitor(bearish), settings).create_session(
            timeframe="M15",
            start_utc=bearish[0].open_time_utc,
            end_utc=sc.window_end(bearish),
        )
    
        assert session_a.engine is not session_b.engine

self = <tests.replay.test_isolation.TestLiveStateIsolation object at 0x000002407F182E70>
make_settings = <function make_settings.<locals>._make at 0x000002400910E840>

    async def test_replay_serves_only_its_own_timeframe_and_window(self, make_settings):
        settings = make_settings()
        candles = sc.impulse_candles()
        service = ReplayService(sc.StubMonitor(candles), settings)
        session = await service.create_session(
            timeframe="M15",
            start_utc=candles[0].open_time_utc,
            end_utc=sc.window_end(candles),
        )
    
        # Nothing is visible before the first step.
        assert session.source.visible_count == 0
        assert (await session.source.get_candles("M15", 300)).count == 0
    
        await service.step(session.session_id)
        series = await session.source.get_candles("M15", 300)
        assert [candle.time for candle in series.candles] == [candles[0].time]
    
        # A different timeframe yields nothing rather than the wrong bars.
        other = await session.source.get_candles("H1", 300)
        assert other.count == 0
    
>       await service.step(session.session_id)

    async def _evaluate_timeframe(self, timeframe: str) -> None:
        """Evaluate a single timeframe against its newest completed candle."""
        tf_state = self._tf_states[timeframe]
    
        # --- candle history -------------------------------------------
        try:
            series = await self._monitor.get_candles(timeframe, self._settings.candle_limit)
        except MarketDataError as exc:
            tf_state.last_error = exc.message
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            tf_state.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            tf_state.data_available = False
            return

    async def test_reconnect_succeeds_when_the_terminal_returns(self):
        fake = FakeMT5(initialize_ok=False)
        connection = MT5Connection(build(reconnect_initial_delay_seconds=0.01), mt5_module=fake)
        await connection.initialize()
        assert connection.is_connected() is False

        await connection.acall(lambda: (_sleep_backoff(connection)))
